# Recall recovery — 2026-09-16 12:27 CST

- **CORE_SYS +1:** [Robion — Efficient Vision-Language-Action Management and Serving for Robot Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12075), **S+**, Routes P1/P2/P3/P4/P6/P8.
- **Why it matters:** direct SLO-aware multi-robot, multi-model VLA serving on multi-GPU edge servers. The serving engine overlaps VLM and ADiT inside each GPU using separate CUDA streams, dynamically restricts VLM SM usage so ADiT can run concurrently, shares streams across co-located models, and prioritizes least-remaining-SLO requests. The management engine supports flexible GPU placement and SLO-aware traffic assignment that concentrates per-model batching while bounding GPU load.
- **Reported results:** 6.7× higher supported robot load than vLLM-Omni and 1.5× than a monolithic VLM+ADiT pipeline within 98% SLO attainment; 8 models on 4 GPUs serve up to 64 robots within 98% SLO attainment.
- **Recall incident:** this paper was submitted 2026-09-10 and should have been surfaced automatically. The previous Physical-AI discovery path relied too heavily on keyword/web queries and did not enforce direct recent-feed enumeration of relevant arXiv categories. The corrected workflow now requires direct `cs.DC`/`cs.AR`/`cs.PF`/`cs.RO` enumeration with `cs.LG` fallback plus reverse-neighborhood checks around ROSA/Kairos/PhyAI/VLA-Perf/vLLM-Omni/M*/Embodied.cpp/vla.cpp/XPU characterization.
- **Canonical state:** 164 works = **118 CORE_SYS / 36 SYS_ALG / 4 ALG / 6 WATCH**. Official PDF is archived privately on the research server; PDFs are not published in this repository. No official implementation repository has been verified yet.
- **Next:** replay the affected 7d→30d Physical-AI systems window with the corrected category census before declaring recall recovered.

- **Route-5 runtime update:** [TeleFuser](https://github.com/Tele-AI/TeleFuser) now documents external [CacheSeek](https://github.com/Tele-AI/CacheSeek) integration for **cross-request approximate latent reuse**. This is distinct from request-local feature cache: it persists reusable latent state across requests and uses prompt-embedding similarity to skip an initial denoising prefix.
- **Serving abstraction:** the integration separates query construction, lookup, resume injection, response capture and save; individual cache failures fall back to uncached execution. The documented backend includes persistent KV/distributed storage plus vector DB + metadata, making this a genuine serving-cache/state substrate rather than a local memoization trick.
